</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 5-65. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Fees. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">The Department shall provide by rule for fees to be paid by applicants and licensees to
cover the reasonable
costs of the Department in administering and enforcing the provisions of this Act.  The Department 
may also provide
by rule for general fees to cover the reasonable expenses of carrying out other
functions and
responsibilities under this Act. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 96-856, eff. 12-31-09</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K5-65.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/5-70</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/5-70)</font></code> <br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 5-70. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Continuing education requirement; managing broker or broker.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a) The requirements of this Section apply to all managing brokers and brokers.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b) Except as otherwise provided in this Section, each person who applies for renewal of a license as a managing broker or broker must successfully complete 12 hours of real estate continuing education courses recommended by the Board and approved by the Department during the current term of the license. In addition, those licensees renewing or obtaining a managing broker's license must successfully complete a 12-hour broker management continuing education course approved by the Department during the current term of the license. The broker management continuing education course must be completed in the classroom, through a live, interactive webinar, or in an online distance education format. No license may be renewed except upon the successful completion of the required courses or their equivalent or upon a waiver of those requirements for good cause shown as determined by the Secretary upon the recommendation of the Board. The requirements of this Article are applicable to all managing brokers and brokers except those managing brokers and brokers who, during the current term of licensure:</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(1) serve in the armed services of the United States;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(2) serve as an elected State or federal official;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(3) serve as a full-time employee of the Department; </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">or</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(4) are admitted to practice law pursuant to Illinois </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Supreme Court rule.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(c) (Blank).</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(d) A person receiving an initial license during the 90 days before the renewal date shall not be required to complete the continuing education courses provided for in subsection (b) of this Section as a condition of initial license renewal. </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(e) The continuing education requirement for brokers and managing brokers shall consist of a single core curriculum, which must include at least 2 credit hours of fair housing training, and an elective curriculum, to be recommended by the Board and approved by the Department in accordance with this subsection. With the exception of the fair housing training, the core curriculum shall not be further divided into subcategories or divisions of instruction. The core curriculum shall consist of 6 total hours during the current term of the license on subjects that may include, but are not limited to, advertising, agency, disclosures, escrow, fair housing, residential leasing agent management, and license law, and must include at least 2 credit hours of fair housing training. The amount of time allotted to each of the remaining subjects shall be recommended by the Board and determined by the Department. </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">The Department, upon the recommendation of the Board, shall review the core curriculum every 4 years, at a minimum, and shall revise the curriculum if necessary. However, the core curriculum's total hourly requirement shall only be subject to change by amendment of this subsection, and any change to the core curriculum shall not be effective for a period of 6 months after such change is made by the Department. The Department shall provide notice to all approved education providers of any changes to the core curriculum. When determining whether revisions of the core curriculum's subjects or specific time requirements are necessary, the Board shall consider recent changes in applicable laws, new laws, and areas of the license law and the Department policy that the Board deems appropriate, and any other subject areas the Board deems timely and applicable in order to prevent violations of this Act and to protect the public. In establishing a recommendation to the Department regarding the elective curriculum, the Board shall consider subjects that cover the various aspects of the practice of real estate that are covered under the scope of this Act. </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(f) The subject areas of continuing education courses recommended by the Board and approved by the Department shall be meant to protect the professionalism of the industry, the consumer, and the public and prevent violations of this Act and may include, without limitation, the following:</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(1) license law and escrow;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(2) antitrust;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(3) fair housing;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(4) agency;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(5) appraisal;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(6) property management;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(7) residential brokerage;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(8) farm property management;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(9) transaction management rights and duties of </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">parties in a transaction;</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(10) commercial brokerage and leasing;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(11) real estate financing;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(12) disclosures;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(13) residential leasing agent management;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(14) advertising; </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(15) broker supervision and designated managing </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">broker responsibility;</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(16) professional conduct;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(17) use of technology; and </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(18) diversity, equity, and inclusion. </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(g) In lieu of credit for those courses listed in subsection (f) of this Section, credit may be earned for serving as a licensed instructor in an approved course of continuing education. The amount of credit earned for teaching a course shall be the amount of continuing education credit for which the course is approved for licensees taking the course.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(h) Credit hours may be earned for self-study programs approved by the Department.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(i) A managing broker or broker may earn credit for a specific continuing education course only once during the current term of the license.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(j) No more than 12 hours of continuing education credit may be taken in one calendar day.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(k) To promote the offering of a uniform and consistent course content, the Department may provide for the development of a single broker management course to be offered by all education providers who choose to offer the broker management continuing education course. The Department may contract for the development of the 12-hour broker management continuing education course with an outside vendor or consultant and, if the course is developed in this manner, the Department or the outside consultant shall license the use of that course to all approved education providers who wish to provide the course. </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(l) Except as specifically provided in this Act, continuing education credit hours may not be earned for completion of pre-license or post-license courses. The courses comprising the approved 45-hour post-license curriculum for broker licensees shall satisfy the continuing education requirement for the initial broker license term. The approved 45-hour managing broker pre-license brokerage administration and management course shall satisfy the 12-hour broker management continuing education requirement for the initial managing broker license term. </font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 102-970, eff. 5-27-22; 103-1039, eff. 1-1-25</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K5-70.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/5-75</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/5-75)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">

</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 5-75. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Out-of-state continuing education credit. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">If a renewal applicant has earned continuing education hours in another state
or territory for which the applicant
 is claiming credit toward full compliance in Illinois, the Department may
approve those hours based upon whether the course is one that
would be approved
under Section 5-70 of this Act, whether the course meets the basic requirements
for
continuing education under
this Act, and any other criteria that are provided by statute or rule.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 103-236, eff. 1-1-24</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K5-75.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/5-80</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/5-80)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">

</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 5-80. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Evidence of compliance with continuing education requirements. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a) Each renewal applicant shall certify, on his or her renewal application,
full compliance with
continuing education requirements set forth in Section 5-70.  The 
education provider shall
retain and submit to the Department after the completion of each course evidence of those
successfully
completing the course as provided by rule.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b) The Department may require additional evidence demonstrating compliance with the
continuing
education requirements.  The renewal applicant shall retain and produce the
evidence of
compliance upon request of the Department.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 100-188, eff. 1-1-18</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K5-80.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/5-85</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">

</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 96-856, eff. 12-31-09. Repealed by P.A. 100-188, eff. 1-1-18.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K5-85.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/Art. 10</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><br>&nbsp;<code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/Art. 10 heading)</font></code> <center><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">ARTICLE 10. </font></code><code> </code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">COMPENSATION AND BUSINESS PRACTICES</font></code></center><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540HArt. 10.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/10-5</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/10-5)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code> <code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 10-5. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Payment of compensation. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a) No licensee shall pay compensation directly to a licensee sponsored by
another sponsoring broker for
the performance of licensed activities.  No licensee sponsored by a broker may
pay compensation to
any licensee other than his or her sponsoring broker for the performance of
licensed activities
unless the licensee paying the compensation is a principal to the transaction.
However, a non-sponsoring broker may pay compensation directly to a licensee
sponsored by
another or a person
who is not sponsored by a broker if the payments are made pursuant to terms of
an employment
agreement that was previously in place between a licensee and the
non-sponsoring broker, and the
payments are for licensed activity performed by that person while previously
sponsored by the now
non-sponsoring broker.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b) No licensee sponsored by a broker shall accept compensation for the
performance of
activities under this Act except from the broker by whom the licensee is
sponsored, except as
provided in this Section.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(c) (Blank).</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(d) One sponsoring broker may pay compensation directly to another
sponsoring broker for the
performance of licensed activities. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(e) Notwithstanding any other provision of this Act, a sponsoring broker may pay compensation to a person currently licensed under the Auction License Act who is in compliance with and providing services under Section 5-32 of this Act. </font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 101-357, eff. 8-9-19.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K10-5.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/10-10</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/10-10)</font></code> <br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 10-10. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Disclosure of compensation. </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a) A licensee must disclose to a client the sponsoring broker's compensation policy, including the terms of compensation and any amounts offered to cooperating brokers who represent other parties in a transaction.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b) A licensee must disclose to a client all sources of compensation related to the transaction received by the licensee from a third party.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(c) If a licensee refers a client to a third party in which the licensee has greater than a 1% ownership interest or from which the licensee receives or may receive dividends or other profit sharing distributions, other than a publicly held or traded company, for the purpose of the client obtaining services related to the transaction, then the licensee shall disclose that fact to the client at the time of making the referral.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(d) If in any one transaction a sponsoring broker receives compensation from both the buyer and seller or lessee and lessor of real estate, the sponsoring broker shall disclose in writing to a client the fact that the compensation is being paid by both buyer and seller or lessee and lessor.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(e) Nothing in the Act shall prohibit the cooperation with or a payment of compensation to an individual domiciled in any other state or country who is licensed as a broker in that individual's state or country of domicile or to a resident of a country that does not require a person to be licensed to act as a broker if the person complies with the laws of the country in which that person resides and practices there as a broker.</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 103-1039, eff. 1-1-25</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K10-10.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/10-15</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/10-15)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code> <code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">

</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 10-15. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">No compensation to persons in violation of Act; compensation
to unlicensed
persons; consumer.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a) No compensation may be paid to any unlicensed person in exchange for the
person
performing licensed activities in violation of this Act.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b) No action or suit shall be instituted, nor recovery therein be had, in
any court of this State
by any person for compensation for any act done or service performed, the doing
or performing of
which is prohibited by this Act to other than licensed managing brokers, brokers,
or residential leasing agents unless
the person was duly
licensed hereunder as a managing broker,
broker, or residential leasing agent under this Act at the time that any such
act was done or service
performed that would give rise to a cause of action for compensation.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(c) A licensee may offer compensation, including prizes, merchandise,
services, rebates,
discounts, or other consideration to an unlicensed person who is a party to a
contract to buy or sell
real estate or is a party to a contract for the lease of real estate, so long
as the offer complies with
the provisions of subdivision (35) of subsection (a) of Section 20-20 of this
Act.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(d) A licensee may offer cash, gifts, prizes, awards, coupons, merchandise,
rebates or chances
to win a game of chance, if not prohibited by any other law or statute,
to a consumer as an
inducement to that consumer to use the services of the licensee even if the
licensee and consumer
do not ultimately enter into a broker-client relationship so long as the offer
complies with the
provisions of subdivision (35) of subsection (a) of Section 20-20 of this Act.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(e)   A licensee shall not pay compensation to an unlicensed person who is not or will not become a party to a real estate transaction in exchange for a referral of real estate services.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(f)   Nothing in this Section shall be construed as waiving or abrogating the provisions of the Real Estate Settlement Procedures Act (RESPA), 88 Stat. 1724. </font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 100-831, eff. 1-1-19; 101-357, eff. 8-9-19.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K10-15.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/10-20</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/10-20)</font></code> <br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 10-20. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sponsoring broker; employment agreement. </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a) A licensee may perform activities as a licensee only for the licensee's sponsoring broker. A licensee must have only one sponsoring broker at any one time.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b) Every broker who employs licensees or has an independent contractor relationship with a licensee shall have a written employment or independent contractor agreement with each such licensee. The broker having this written employment or independent contractor agreement with the licensee must be that licensee's sponsoring broker.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(c) Every sponsoring broker must have a written employment or independent contractor agreement with each licensee the broker sponsors. The agreement shall address the employment or independent contractor relationship terms, including without limitation supervision, duties, compensation, and termination process.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(d) (Blank).</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(d-5) If a written agreement provides for an independent contractor relationship that clearly states and establishes that relationship, the specific provisions of this Act shall control for licensee's conduct of brokerage activities. </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(e) Notwithstanding the fact that a sponsoring broker has an employment or independent contractor agreement with a licensee, a sponsoring broker may pay compensation directly to a business entity solely owned by that licensee that has been formed for the purpose of receiving compensation earned by the licensee. A business entity that receives compensation from a sponsoring broker as provided for in this subsection (e) shall not be required to be licensed under this Act and must either be owned solely by the licensee or by the licensee together with the licensee's spouse, but only if the spouse and licensee are both licensed and sponsored by the same sponsoring broker or the spouse is not also licensed.</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 103-1039, eff. 1-1-25</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K10-20.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/10-25</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/10-25)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code> <code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">

</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 10-25. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Expiration of brokerage agreement. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">No licensee shall obtain any written brokerage agreement that does not either provide
for automatic
expiration within a definite period of time, and if longer than one year, provide the client with a right to terminate the agreement annually by giving no more than 30 days' prior written notice.  Any written brokerage agreement not containing such a provision
 shall be void.  When the license of any sponsoring broker is
suspended or revoked,  any
brokerage agreement with the sponsoring broker shall be deemed to expire upon
the effective date
of the suspension or revocation.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 103-236, eff. 1-1-24</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K10-25.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/10-27</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
			</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
				</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/10-27)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
				</font></code> <code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 10-27. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Disclosure of licensee status. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Each licensee shall disclose,
in writing, his or her status as a licensee to all parties in a transaction
when the licensee is selling, leasing, or purchasing any interest, direct or
indirect, in the real estate that is the subject of the transaction.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 91-245, eff. 12-31-99</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K10-27.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/10-30</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/10-30)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code> <code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">

</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 10-30. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Advertising.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a) No advertising, whether in print, via the Internet, or through social media, digital forums, or any other media, shall be fraudulent, deceptive, inherently misleading, or
proven to be
misleading in practice. Advertising shall be considered misleading or untruthful if,
when taken as a whole,
there is a distinct and reasonable possibility that it will be misunderstood or
will deceive the
ordinary consumer.  Advertising shall
contain all information
necessary to communicate the information contained therein to the public in an accurate,
direct, and readily
comprehensible manner. Team names may not contain inherently misleading terms, such as "company", "realty", "real estate", "agency", "associates", "brokers", "properties", or "property". </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b) No blind advertisements may be used
by any licensee, in any media, except as provided for in this Section.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(c) A licensee shall disclose, in writing, to all parties in a transaction the licensee's
 status as a
licensee and any and all interest the licensee has or may have in the real
estate constituting the
subject matter thereof, directly or indirectly, according to the following
guidelines:</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(1) On broker yard signs or in broker advertisements, </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">no disclosure of ownership is necessary.  However, the ownership shall be indicated on any property data form accessible to the consumer and disclosed to persons responding to any advertisement or any sign.  The term "broker owned" or "agent owned" is sufficient disclosure.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(2) A sponsored or inactive licensee selling or </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">leasing property, owned solely by the sponsored or inactive licensee, without utilizing brokerage services of their sponsoring broker or any other licensee, may advertise "By Owner".  For purposes of this Section, property is "solely owned" by a sponsored or inactive licensee if the licensee (i) has a 100% ownership interest alone, (ii) has ownership as a joint tenant or tenant by the entirety, or (iii) holds a 100% beneficial interest in a land trust.  Sponsored or inactive licensees selling or leasing "By Owner" shall comply with the following if advertising by owner:</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(A) On "By Owner" yard signs, the sponsored or </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">inactive licensee shall indicate "broker owned" or "agent owned."  "By Owner" advertisements used in any medium of advertising shall include the term "broker owned" or "agent owned."</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(B) If a sponsored or inactive licensee runs </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">advertisements, for the purpose of purchasing or leasing real estate, the licensee shall disclose in the advertisements the licensee's status as a licensee.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(C) A sponsored or inactive licensee shall not </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">use the sponsoring broker's name or the sponsoring broker's company name in connection with the sale, lease, or advertisement of the property nor utilize the sponsoring broker's or company's name in connection with the sale, lease, or advertising of the property in a manner likely to create confusion among the public as to whether or not the services of a real estate company are being utilized or whether or not a real estate company has an ownership interest in the property.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(d) A sponsored licensee may not advertise under the licensee's own name.
Advertising in any media shall be
under the direct supervision of the sponsoring or designated managing broker and in the
sponsoring broker's
business name, which in the case of a franchise shall include the franchise
affiliation as well as the
name of the individual firm.
 This provision does not apply under the following circumstances:</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(1) When a licensee enters into a brokerage agreement </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">relating to  real estate owned by the licensee, or real estate in which the licensee has an ownership interest, with another licensed broker; or</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(2) When a licensee is selling or leasing real estate </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">owned by the licensee or buying or leasing real estate for their own use, after providing the appropriate written disclosure of ownership interest as required in paragraph (2) of subsection (c) of this Section.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(e) No licensee shall list the licensee's name or otherwise advertise in the licensee's own name to the
general public through any
medium of advertising as being in the real estate business without listing the sponsoring
broker's business name.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(f) The sponsoring broker's business name and the name of the licensee must
appear in all
advertisements, including business cards. In advertising that includes the sponsoring broker's name and a team name or individual broker's name, the sponsoring broker's business name shall be at least equal in size or larger than the team name or that of the individual.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(g) Those individuals licensed as a managing broker and designated with the Department as a designated managing broker by their sponsoring broker shall identify themselves to the public in advertising, except on "For Sale" or similar signs, as a designated managing broker.  No other individuals holding a managing broker's license may hold themselves out to the public or other licensees as a designated managing broker, but they may hold themselves out to be a managing broker. </font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 103-236, eff. 1-1-24</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K10-30.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/10-35</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/10-35)</font></code> <br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 10-35. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Internet and related advertising.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New"> </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a) Licensees intending to sell or share consumer information gathered from or through the Internet or other electronic communication media, including, but not limited to, social media and digital forums, shall disclose that intention to consumers in a timely and readily apparent manner.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b) A licensee using Internet or other similar electronic advertising media must not:</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(1) use a URL or domain name that is deceptive or </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">misleading;</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(2) deceptively or without authorization frame </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">another sponsoring broker's or multiple listing service website; or</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(3) engage in phishing or the deceptive use of </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">metatags, keywords or other devices and methods to direct, drive or divert Internet traffic or otherwise mislead consumers. </font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 101-357, eff. 8-9-19.)</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K10-35.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/10-40</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/10-40)</font></code> <br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 10-40. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Company policy. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">   Every brokerage company or entity, other than a sole proprietorship with no other sponsored licensees, shall adopt a company or office policy dealing with topics such as:</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(1) the agency policy of the entity;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(2) fair housing, nondiscrimination and harassment;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(3) confidentiality of client information;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(4) advertising;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(5) training and supervision of sponsored licensees;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(6) required disclosures and use of forms;</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(7) handling of risk management matters; and</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(8) handling of earnest money and escrows. </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">These topics are provided as an example and are not intended to be inclusive or exclusive of other topics. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 96-856, eff. 12-31-09</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K10-40.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/10-45</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/10-45)</font></code> <br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 10-45. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Broker price opinions and comparative market analyses.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New"> </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a) A broker price opinion or comparative market analysis may be prepared or provided by a real estate broker or managing broker for any of the following:</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(1) an existing or potential buyer or seller of an </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2"
