href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K10-45.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/10-50</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/10-50)</font></code> <br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 10-50. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Guaranteed sales plans.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a)   As used in this Section, a "guaranteed sales plan" means a real estate purchase or sales plan whereby a licensee enters into one or more conditional or unconditional written contracts with a seller, one of which is a brokerage agreement, and wherein the person agrees to purchase the seller's property within a specified period of time, at a specific price, in the event the property is not sold in accordance with the terms of a brokerage agreement to be entered into between the sponsoring broker and the seller.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b)   A person who offers a guaranteed sales plan to consumers is engaged in licensed activity under this Act and is required to have a license.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(c)   A licensee offering a guaranteed sales plan shall provide the details, including the purchase price, and conditions of the plan, in writing to the party to whom the plan is offered prior to entering into the brokerage agreement.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(d)   A licensee offering a guaranteed sales plan shall provide to the party to whom the plan is offered evidence of sufficient financial resources to satisfy the commitment to purchase undertaken by the broker in the plan.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(e)   A licensee offering a guaranteed sales plan shall undertake to market the property of the seller subject to the plan in the same manner in which the broker would market any other property, unless the agreement with the seller provides otherwise.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(f)   The licensee may not purchase seller's property until the period for offering the property for sale has ended according to its terms or is otherwise terminated.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(g)   Any licensee who fails to perform on a guaranteed sales plan in strict accordance with its terms shall be subject to all the penalties provided in this Act for violations thereof and, in addition, shall be subject to a civil fine payable to the party injured by the default in an amount of up to $25,000.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 101-357, eff. 8-9-19.)</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K10-50.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/10-55</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/10-55)</font></code> <br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 10-55. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Designated managing broker responsibility and supervision.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a)    A designated managing broker shall be responsible for the supervision of all licensees associated with a designated managing broker's office.  A designated managing broker's responsibilities include implementation of company policies, the training of licensees and other employees on the company's policies as well as on relevant provisions of this Act, and providing assistance to all licensees in real estate transactions. The designated managing broker shall be responsible for, and shall supervise, all special accounts of the company.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b)   A designated managing broker's responsibilities shall further include directly handling all earnest money, escrows, and contract negotiations for all transactions where the designated agent for the transaction has not completed his or her 45 hours of post-license education, as well as the approval of all advertisements involving a licensee who has not completed his or her 45 hours of post-license education. Licensees that have not completed their 45 hours of post-license education shall have no authority to bind the sponsoring broker.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 101-357, eff. 8-9-19.)</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K10-55.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/Art. 15</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><br>&nbsp;<code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/Art. 15 heading)</font></code> <center><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">ARTICLE 15. </font></code><code> </code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">AGENCY RELATIONSHIPS</font></code></center><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540HArt. 15.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/15-5</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/15-5)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code> <code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 15-5. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Legislative intent. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a) The General Assembly finds that application of the common law of agency
to the
relationships among licensees under this Act and consumers of real
estate brokerage
services has resulted in misunderstandings and consequences that have been
contrary to the best
interests of the public.  The General Assembly further finds that the real
estate brokerage industry
has a significant impact upon the economy of the State of Illinois and that it is in the best interest of
the public to provide codification of the relationships between licensees under this Act 
and consumers of real estate brokerage services in order to prevent detrimental misunderstandings
and misinterpretations of the relationships by consumers, managing brokers, and brokers and
thus promote and provide stability in the real estate market.  This Article 15
is enacted to govern the
relationships between consumers of real estate brokerage services and licensees under this Act
 to the extent not governed by an individual written
agreement between a
sponsoring broker and a consumer, providing that
there is a relationship other than designated agency.
This
Article 15 applies to
the exclusion of the common law concepts of principal and agent and to the
fiduciary duties, which
have been applied to managing brokers, brokers, and real estate
brokerage services.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b) The General Assembly further finds that this Article 15 is not intended
to
prescribe or affect
contractual relationships between managing brokers and brokers and the broker's
affiliated licensees.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(c) This Article 15 may serve as a basis for private rights of action and
defenses by sellers, buyers,
landlords, tenants, managing brokers, and brokers.  The
private rights of action,
however, do not extend to any other Articles
of this
Act.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 101-357, eff. 8-9-19.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K15-5.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/15-10</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/15-10)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code> <code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 15-10. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Relationships between licensees and consumers. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Licensees shall be considered to be representing the consumer they are working
with as a
designated agent for the consumer unless there is a written agreement between the sponsoring broker and the consumer providing that there is a different relationship.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">


</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 101-357, eff. 8-9-19.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K15-10.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/15-15</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/15-15)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code> <code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 15-15. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Duties of licensees representing clients. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a) A licensee representing a client shall:</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(1) Perform the terms of the brokerage agreement </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">between a broker and the client.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(2) Promote the best interest of the client by:</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(A) Seeking a transaction at the price and terms </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">stated in the brokerage agreement or at a price and terms otherwise acceptable to the client.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(B) Timely presenting all offers to and from the </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">client, unless the client has waived this duty.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(C) Disclosing to the client material facts </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">concerning the transaction of which the licensee has actual knowledge, unless that information is confidential information.  Material facts do not include the following when located on or related to real estate that is not the subject of the transaction: (i) physical conditions that do not have a substantial adverse effect on the value of the real estate, (ii) fact situations, or (iii) occurrences and acts at the property.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(D) Timely accounting for all money and property </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">received in which the client has, may have, or should have had an interest.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(E) Obeying specific directions of the client </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">that are not otherwise contrary to applicable statutes, ordinances, or rules.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(F) Acting in a manner consistent with promoting </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">the client's best interests as opposed to a licensee's or any other person's self-interest.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(3) Exercise reasonable skill and care in the </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">performance of brokerage services.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(4) Keep confidential all confidential information </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">received from the client.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(5) Comply with all requirements of this Act and all </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">applicable statutes and regulations, including without limitation fair housing and civil rights statutes.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b) A licensee representing a client does not breach a duty or obligation to
the client by
showing alternative properties to prospective buyers or tenants, by showing
properties in which
the client is interested to other prospective buyers or tenants, or by making or preparing contemporaneous offers or contracts to purchase or lease the same property.  However, a licensee shall provide written disclosure to all clients for whom the licensee is preparing or making contemporaneous offers or contracts to purchase or lease the same property and shall refer to another designated agent any client that requests such referral.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(c) A licensee representing a buyer or tenant client will not be presumed to
have breached a
duty or obligation to that client by working on the basis that the licensee
will receive a higher fee or
compensation based on higher selling price or lease cost.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(d) A licensee shall not be liable to a client for providing false
information to the client if the
false information was provided to the licensee by a customer unless the
licensee knew or should
have known the information was false.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(e) Nothing in the Section shall be construed as changing a licensee's duty
under common law
as to negligent or fraudulent misrepresentation of material information.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 101-357, eff. 8-9-19.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K15-15.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/15-20</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
			</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
				</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/15-20)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
				</font></code> <code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 15-20. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Failure to disclose information not affecting physical
condition.  No cause of action shall arise against a licensee for the failure
to disclose:
(i) that an occupant of the property was afflicted with Human Immunodeficiency
Virus (HIV) or any other medical condition;
(ii) that the property was the site of an act or occurrence that had no effect
on the physical condition of the property or its environment or the structures
located thereon;
(iii) fact situations on property that is not the subject of the transaction;
or
(iv) physical conditions located on property that is not the subject of the
transaction that do not have a substantial adverse effect on the value of the
real estate that is the subject of the transaction.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 91-245, eff. 12-31-99; 92-16, eff. 6-28-01</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K15-20.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/15-25</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/15-25)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code> <code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 15-25. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Licensee's relationship with customers. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Licensees shall treat all customers honestly and shall not negligently
or knowingly give
them false information.  A licensee engaged by a seller client shall timely
disclose to customers
who are prospective buyers all latent material adverse facts pertaining to the
physical condition of
the property that are actually known by the licensee and that could not be
discovered by a
reasonably diligent inspection of the property by the customer.  A licensee
shall not be liable to a
customer for providing false information to the customer if the false
information was provided to
the licensee by the licensee's client and the licensee did not have actual
knowledge that the
information was false.  No cause of action shall arise on behalf of any person
against a licensee for
revealing information in compliance with this Section.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">

</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 101-357, eff. 8-9-19.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K15-25.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/15-30</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">

		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 15-30. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Duties after termination of brokerage agreement. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Except as may be provided in a written agreement between the broker and the
client, neither a
sponsoring broker nor any licensee affiliated with the sponsoring broker owes
any further duties to
the client after termination, expiration, or completion of performance of the
brokerage agreement,
except:</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(1) to account for all moneys and property relating </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">to the transaction; and</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(2) to keep confidential all confidential information </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">received during the course of the brokerage agreement.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 91-245, eff. 12-31-99</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K15-30.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/15-35</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/15-35)</font></code> <br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 15-35. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Agency relationship disclosure. </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a) A licensee acting as a designated agent shall advise a consumer in writing, no later than beginning to work as a designated agent on behalf of the consumer, of the following:</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(1) That a designated agency relationship exists, </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">unless there is written agreement between the sponsoring broker and the consumer providing for a different agency relationship; and</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(2) The name or names of the designated agent or </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">agents on the written disclosure, which must be included as part of a brokerage agreement or be a separate document, a copy of which is retained by the sponsoring broker for the licensee and company records, and a copy of which must be provided to the consumer or client.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b) The licensee representing the consumer shall discuss with the consumer the sponsoring broker's compensation policy, including the terms of compensation and any amounts offered to cooperating brokers who represent other parties in a transaction.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(c) A licensee shall disclose in writing to a customer that the licensee is not acting as the agent of the customer at a time intended to prevent disclosure of confidential information from a customer to a licensee, but in no event later than the preparation of an offer to purchase or lease real property.</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 102-970, eff. 5-27-22; 103-1039, eff. 1-1-25</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K15-35.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/15-40</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
			</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
				</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/15-40)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
				</font></code> <code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 15-40. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Compensation does not determine agency. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Compensation does not determine agency relationship.  The payment or promise of
payment of
compensation to a licensee is not determinative of whether an agency
relationship has been created
between any licensee and a consumer.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 91-245, eff. 12-31-99</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K15-40.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/15-45</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/15-45)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">

</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 15-45. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Dual agency. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a) An individual licensee may act as a dual agent or a sponsoring broker may permit one or more of its sponsored licensees to act as dual agents in the same transaction only with the informed written
consent of all clients.
Informed written consent shall be presumed to have been given by any client who
signs a document
that includes the following:</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">"The undersigned (insert name(s)), ("Licensee"), may </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">undertake a dual representation (represent both the seller or landlord and the buyer or tenant) for the sale or lease of property.  The undersigned acknowledge they were informed of the possibility of this type of representation. Before signing this document please read the following: Representing more than one party to a transaction presents a conflict of interest since both clients may rely upon Licensee's advice and the client's respective interests may be adverse to each other. Licensee will undertake this representation only with the written consent of ALL clients in the transaction. Any agreement between the clients as to a final contract price and other terms is a result of negotiations between the clients acting in their own best interests and on their own behalf.  You acknowledge that Licensee has explained the implications of dual representation, including the risks involved, and understand that you have been advised to seek independent advice from your advisors or attorneys before signing any documents in this transaction.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><center><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">WHAT A LICENSEE CAN DO FOR CLIENTS</font></code></center><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">

</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">1. Treat all clients honestly.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">2. Provide information about the property to the buyer or </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">tenant.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">3. Disclose all latent material defects in the property </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">that are known to the Licensee.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">4. Disclose financial qualification of the buyer or </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">tenant to the seller or landlord.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">5. Explain real estate terms.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">6. Help the buyer or tenant to arrange for property </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">inspections.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">7. Explain closing costs and procedures.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">8. Help the buyer compare financing alternatives.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">9. Provide information about comparable properties that </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">have sold so both clients may make educated decisions on what price to accept or offer.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><center><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">WHAT LICENSEE CANNOT DISCLOSE TO CLIENTS WHEN</font></code></center><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><center><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">ACTING AS A DUAL AGENT</font></code></center><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">1. Confidential information that Licensee may know about </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">a client, without that client's permission.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">2. The price or terms the seller or landlord will take </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">other than the listing price without permission of the seller or landlord.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">3. The price or terms the buyer or tenant is willing to </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">pay without permission of the buyer or tenant.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">4. A recommended or suggested price or terms the buyer or </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">tenant should offer.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">5. A recommended or suggested price or terms the seller </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">or landlord should counter with or accept.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">If either client is uncomfortable with this </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">disclosure and dual representation, please let Licensee know. You are not required to sign this document unless you want to allow Licensee to proceed as a Dual Agent in this transaction. By signing below, you acknowledge that you have read and understand this form and voluntarily consent to Licensee acting as a Dual Agent (that is, to represent BOTH the seller or landlord and the buyer or tenant) should that become necessary."</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b) The dual agency disclosure form provided for in subsection (a) of this
Section must be
presented by a licensee, who offers dual representation, to the client at the
time the brokerage
agreement is entered into and may be signed by the client at that time or at
any time before the
licensee acts as a dual agent as to the client.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(c) A licensee acting in a dual agency capacity in a transaction must obtain
a written
confirmation from the licensee's clients of their prior consent for the
licensee to
act as a dual agent in the
transaction.
This confirmation should be obtained at the time the clients are executing any
offer or contract to
purchase or lease in a transaction in which the licensee is acting as a dual
agent.  This confirmation
may be included in another document, such as a contract to purchase, in which
case the client must
not only sign the document but also initial the confirmation of dual agency
provision.  That
confirmation must state, at a minimum, the following:</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">"The undersigned confirm that they have previously </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">consented to (insert name(s)), ("Licensee"), acting as a Dual Agent in providing brokerage services on their behalf and specifically consent to Licensee acting as a Dual Agent in regard to the transaction referred to in this document."</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(d) No cause of action shall arise on behalf of any person against a dual
agent for making
disclosures allowed or required by this Article, and the dual agent does not
terminate any agency
relationship by making the allowed or required disclosures.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(e) In the case of dual agency, each client and the licensee possess only
actual knowledge and
information.  There shall be no imputation of knowledge or information among or
between clients,
brokers, or their affiliated licensees.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(f) In any transaction, a licensee may without liability withdraw from
representing a client who
has not consented to a disclosed dual agency.  The withdrawal shall not
prejudice the ability of the
licensee to continue to represent the other client in the transaction or limit
the licensee from
representing the client in other transactions.  When a withdrawal as
contemplated in this subsection
(f) occurs, the licensee shall not receive a referral fee for referring a
client to another licensee unless
written disclosure is made to both the withdrawing client and the client that
continues to be
represented by the licensee.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(g)    A licensee shall not serve as a dual agent in any transaction when the licensee, or an entity in which the licensee has or will have any ownership interest, is a party to the transaction.</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 101-357, eff. 8-9-19; 102-970, eff. 5-27-22.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K15-45.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/15-50</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/15-50)</font></code> <br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 15-50. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Brokerage agreements; designated agency. </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a) A sponsoring broker entering into a brokerage relationship with any person for the listing of property or for the purpose of representing any person in the buying, selling, exchanging, renting, or leasing of real estate shall set forth the terms of that relationship in a written brokerage agreement. The brokerage agreement shall specifically designate those licensees employed by or affiliated with the sponsoring broker who will be acting as legal agents of that person to the exclusion of all other licensees employed by or affiliated with the sponsoring broker. A sponsoring broker entering into an agreement under the provisions of this Section shall not be considered to be acting for more than one party in a transaction if the licensees are specifically designated as legal agents of a person and are not representing more than one party in a transaction.</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a-5) Nothing in this Section prevents a client from seeking to enforce an oral agreement. The absence of a written agreement does not create an affirmative defense (i) to the existence, or lack thereof, of an agreement between the parties; or (ii) as to whether licensed activity was performed under this Act. This Section does not prevent a court from imposing legal or equitable remedies. </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b) A sponsoring broker designating affiliated licensees to act as agents of clients shall take ordinary and necessary care to protect confidential information disclosed by a client to the designated agent. </font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(c) A designated agent may disclose to the designated agent's sponsoring broker or persons specified by the sponsoring broker confidential information of a client for the purpose of seeking advice or assistance for the benefit of the client in regard to a possible transaction. Confidential information shall not be disclosed by the sponsoring broker or other specified representative of the sponsoring broker unless otherwise required by this Act or requested or permitted by the client who originally disclosed the confidential information.</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 103-1039, eff. 1-1-25</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K15-50.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/15-55</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
			</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
				</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/15-55)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
				</font></code> <code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 15-55. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">No subagency. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">A broker is not considered to be a subagent of a client of another broker
solely by reason of
membership or other affiliation by the brokers in a multiple listing service or
other similar
information source, and an offer of subagency may not be made through a
multiple listing service
or other similar information source.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 91-245, eff. 12-31-99</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K15-55.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/15-60</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
			</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
				</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/15-60)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
				</font></code> <code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 15-60. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Vicarious liability. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">A consumer shall not be vicariously liable for the acts or omissions of a
licensee in providing
licensed activities for or on behalf of the consumer.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 91-245, eff. 12-31-99</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K15-60.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/15-65</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/15-65)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code> <code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 15-65. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Regulatory enforcement. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Nothing contained in this Article limits the Department in its regulation of licensees
under other Articles of
this Act and the substantive rules adopted by the Department.  The Department is
authorized to adopt any rules that may be necessary for the implementation
and enforcement
of this Article 15.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 101-357, eff. 8-9-19.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K15-65.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/15-70</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
			</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
				</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/15-70)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
				</font></code> <code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 15-70. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Actions for damages. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a) In any action brought under this Article 15, the court may, in its
discretion, award only
actual damages and court costs or grant injunctive relief, when appropriate.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b) Any action under this Article 15 shall be forever barred unless
commenced within 2
years after the person bringing the action knew or should reasonably have known
of such act or
omission.  In no event shall the action be brought more than 5 years after the
date on which
the act or omission occurred.  If the person entitled to bring the action is
under the age of 18 or
under legal disability the period of limitations shall not begin to run until
the disability is removed.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
		</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 91-245, eff. 12-31-99</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K15-70.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/15-75</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/15-75)</font></code> <br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 15-75. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Exclusive brokerage agreements. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">All exclusive
brokerage agreements must be in writing and specify that the sponsoring broker, through one or more sponsored licensees, must provide, at a minimum, the following services:</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(1) accept delivery of and present to the client </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">offers and counteroffers to buy, sell, or lease the client's property or the property the client seeks to purchase or lease;</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(2) assist the client in developing, communicating, </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">negotiating, and presenting offers, counteroffers, and notices that relate to the offers and counteroffers until a lease or purchase agreement is signed and all contingencies are satisfied or waived; and</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(3) answer the client's questions relating to the </font></code></div></td></tr><tr><td><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="100%"><tr><td width="1"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</font></code></td><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">offers, counteroffers, notices, and contingencies.</font></code></div></td></tr></table></td></tr><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 101-357, eff. 8-9-19.)</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540K15-75.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/Art. 20</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
	</font></code><br>&nbsp;<code><font size="2" face="Courier New">

		</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">DISCIPLINARY PROVISIONS</font></code></center><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">

</font></code></div></td></tr></table></body></html><br><a style="visibility: hidden" href='/legislation/ilcs/documents/022504540HArt. 20.htm'></a><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN"><html><head><title>225 ILCS 454/20-5</title></head><body><table align="center" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" width="500"><tr><td><div align="justify"><p></p><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">

</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(225 ILCS 454/20-5)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code> <code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Section scheduled to be repealed on January 1, 2030)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 20-5. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Index of decisions. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">The Department shall maintain an index of formal decisions regarding the issuance,
refusal to issue, renewal,
refusal to renew, revocation, and suspension of licenses and probationary or
other disciplinary
action taken under this Act.  The
index shall be available to the public during regular business hours.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 101-357, eff. 8-9-19.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">

</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">

</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Sec. 20-10. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">Unlicensed practice; civil penalty. </font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(a) Any person who practices, offers to practice, attempts to practice, or
holds oneself out to
practice as a managing broker, broker, or residential leasing agent
without being licensed under
this Act shall, in addition to any other penalty provided by law, pay a civil penalty
 to the Department in an
amount not to exceed $25,000 for each offense as determined by the Department.  The civil penalty
 shall be
assessed by the Department after a hearing is held in accordance with the provisions set
forth in this Act
regarding the provision of a hearing for the discipline of a license.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(b) The Department has the authority and power to investigate any and all unlicensed
activity.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(c) The civil penalty shall be paid within 60 days after the effective date of
the order imposing the
civil penalty.  The order shall constitute a judgment and may be filed and
execution had thereon in the
same manner from any court of record.</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
</font></code><br><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">(Source: P.A. 101-357, eff. 8-9-19.)</font></code><code><font size="2" face="Courier New">
